II. Software Requirement Specification
1. Overall Requirements
1.1 Context Diagram
The context diagram illustrates the overall interaction between the EnglishLab system and its main actors, including Learners, Teacher, Content Manager, Staff, Manager, and Admin, along with external services such as AI Engine Service, Payment Service, Email Service, and Google Meet Service. The system serves as a centralized platform for managing online learning, classroom activities, assessments, learning content, scheduling, and administrative operations. Each role interacts with the system according to its responsibilities to support a complete English learning and classroom management environment.
1.2 Main Workflows
1.2.1 Enroll Online Course Workflow
The Course Enrollment and Payment Workflow starts with the Learner browsing available courses and viewing the system-displayed course catalog to see specific course details before attempting to add a selected course to their cart. The system first verifies if the Learner has already registered for this course, displaying a registration confirmation and returning them to the catalog if true, but if false, the Learner views their cart and proceeds to checkout, provided there are registered courses in the cart. During checkout, the system generates a payment request that is sent to the Payment Gateway for processing, and if the initial payment verification fails, the system requests another status check, ultimately displaying an error message and redirecting the Learner back if the payment fails again. However, upon a successful payment verification, the system grants course access, enabling the Learner to access the course, learn the lessons while their progress is tracked, take lesson practices whose results are recorded, and finally complete the course to receive an officially issued completion status.
1.2.2 Take Placement Test Workflow
The Placement Exam Workflow details the process of a Learner taking a timed practice test, beginning with the Learner choosing to take the placement exam, which prompts the EnglishLab System to load the necessary exam content. Once the Learner starts the exam, the system simultaneously starts a countdown timer and enters an interruptible activity region where the Learner cycles through viewing a displayed question, answering it, and having the system auto-save the answers until no questions remain, at which point the Learner manually submits the exam. Crucially, if the Learner runs out of time before finishing, a time event is triggered that instantly interrupts the question-answering loop and forces the system to execute an auto-submit action. Regardless of whether the exam was submitted manually by the Learner or automatically due to the timer expiring, the workflow converges as the system calculates the final score, generates a detailed result report, and presents it to the Learner for review before the process concludes.
1.2.3 Enroll Classroom Workflow
The Enroll Classroom Workflow initiates when the EnglishLab System creates a class, generates a corresponding class record, assigns a timetable, and assigns a teacher to the course. Following these setup steps, the workflow splits into two parallel tracks where the system simultaneously notifies the Teacher, who then views their assigned class and waits for the session to start, and checks whether the class is full or registration is closed. If the class is still open for registration, the system enrolls the Learner, sends them a notification, and allows them to view both the class information and the timetable, eventually merging this track with the Teacher's track. Once both parties are ready, the parallel tracks synchronize as the Teacher conducts the class session while the Learner joins the learning activities, bringing the entire classroom training process to its completion.
1.2.4 Take Practice & Homework Workflow
The Practice and Homework Workflow within the EnglishLab system begins with the Teacher deciding to manage either homework or practice activities. If Homework Management is selected, the Teacher creates and assigns a homework task, allowing the Learner to access and submit their work, which the system stores so the Teacher can review and grade the submission, prompting the system to store the final grades for the Learner to view before the process ends. Alternatively, if Practice Management is chosen, the Teacher creates a practice activity by selecting a format such as 4 Skills or Flashcard, which is then published by the system so the Learner can access learning resources, choose the practice activity, and complete the selected format. Once completed, the system automatically grades the practice using AI, enabling the Teacher to review the results and provide feedback that the Learner can subsequently view to conclude the workflow.
1.3 User Requirements
1.3.1 Main Actors
#
Actor
Type
Description
1
Guest
Concrete
An individual who accesses the platform to explore public information, such as the homepage, course catalog, and marketing materials. This role allows guests to register to become an authenticated user.
2
System User
Abstract
An abstract actor representing any user who has successfully authenticated (logged in) to the system. This role encapsulates common functionalities such as managing personal profiles, changing passwords, and viewing notifications.
3
Learner (Student)
Concrete
Learners participate in classroom learning, enroll in online courses, access learning materials, complete assignments and quizzes, and track their learning progress.
4
Teacher
Concrete
Teachers manage classroom activities, attendance, assignments, quizzes, grading, and monitor learner performance during teaching activities.
5
Content Manager
Concrete
Content managers maintain and organize learning materials, online courses, Curriculumnes, flashcards, and placement examination content within the system.
6
Staff
Concrete
Staffs handle teacher scheduling, classroom assignment, student enrollment into classes, and coordination of teaching operations.
7
Manager
Concrete
Managers oversee classroom operations, teacher performance, learner feedback, operational reports, and revenue analytics for educational management purposes.
8
Admin
Concrete
System administrators manage user accounts, access control, security settings, notifications, and overall system configuration.
9
Email Service
External / Service
Automated email notification service (SMTP / JavaMailSender) delivering OTP verification codes, password reset links, course payment invoices, learning reminders, and broadcast announcements.
10
Google Meet Service
External / Service
Virtual classroom meeting service providing live video conferencing sessions and session participation tracking for online classrooms.
11
Payment Service (PayOS)
External / Service
Online payment gateway facilitating QR code checkout, transaction processing, and automated Webhook payment confirmation.
12
AI Engine Service
External / Service
Artificial intelligence engine (OpenAI / Gemini) providing automated placement test evaluation (Speaking/Writing), pronunciation scoring, essay auto-grading, and weak skill analytics.

1.5 Entity Relationship Diagram
1.5.1 Authentication
Entities Description
#
Entity
Description
1
users
Stores account and profile information for every platform user, including the directly stored login role.
2
roles
Stores the set of assignable system roles used for access control.
3
user_roles
Records which roles are assigned to which users.
4
auth_tokens
Stores authentication and verification tokens such as email-verification and password-reset tokens.
5
teacher_profiles
Stores extended teaching profile details for users who act as teachers.
1.5.2 Product Package & Delivery Models
Entities Description
#
Entity
Description
1
package_types
Stores the catalog classification of products such as online or classroom.
2
packages
Stores the commercial product/offering layer with pricing, status, and sales information.
3
package_availability
Stores the sales window, access duration, and capacity rules for a package.
4
course_categories
Stores categories used to group and filter online courses.
5
online_courses
Stores the self-paced online delivery details linked to an online package.
6
classroom_offerings
Stores a scheduled class offering whose delivery_mode distinguishes offline (campus) from virtual (Google Meet) classes.
7
campuses
Stores physical branch or campus locations.
8
classroom_rooms
Stores physical rooms belonging to a campus.

Description

2. Use Case Specifications
2.1 Common
2.1.1 Browse Public Online Courses
UC ID and Name:
UC-02 Browse Public Online Courses
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
03/06/2026
Primary Actor:
All actors
Secondary Actors:
None
Description:
As a user, I want to browse, search, and view the catalog of courses available to the public so that I can explore what the system offers before registering.
Trigger:
The user opens the “Khóa học” (Courses) page (/courses).
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The course catalog is publicly accessible without logging in.
Postconditions:
POST-1. The list of active, published courses matching the criteria is displayed.
POST-2. No data is changed in the system (read-only use case).
Normal Flow:
1. The user opens the “Khóa học” (Courses) page.
2. The user enters keywords in the search box (“Nhập tên khóa học...”) or selects a category/level filter.
3. System retrieves the active, published courses matching the criteria.
4. System displays the list of courses with thumbnail, title, level, and price.
5. The user clicks “Xem chi tiết khóa học” (View course details) to open a course detail page.
Alternative Flows:
2.A1 No results found
System finds no courses matching the criteria and displays MSG-04.
The user clears or changes the filters (“Xóa tất cả bộ lọc”) and continues from step 2.
Exceptions:
None
Priority:
Medium
Frequency of Use:
Very High

2.1.5 Submit Support Request
UC ID and Name:
UC-12 Submit Support Request
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
03/06/2026
Primary Actor:
System user
Secondary Actors:
None
Description:
As a user, I want to submit a support request or report an issue so that I can get help from the administration. .
Trigger:
The user opens “Hỗ trợ” (Support) and clicks “Gửi phiếu hỗ trợ” (Submit support ticket).
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The user must be successfully logged in and have a valid account in the system.
PRE-2. The user must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The user must have a verified email.
Postconditions:
POST-1. A new support ticket is created in the database with status “Open”.
POST-2. The ticket appears in the support queue for staff to resolve (UC-44).
Normal Flow:
1. System displays the support ticket form.
2. The user enters a Title and Description and selects a Category.
3. The user clicks “Gửi” (Submit).
4. System validates the required fields and saves the ticket with status “Open”.
5. System displays MSG-03.
Alternative Flows:
A1. Missing required fields
System detects an empty required field and blocks submission with an inline validation message; the user completes the fields and continues from step 3.
A2. Resubmit duplicate ticket
The user already has a similar ticket created within the last 24 hours
The system detects the duplicate.
The system displays a message to the user.
User select:
View my ticket: Navigate to the previous ticket's detail page.
Submit anyway: Continues to create a new ticket (step 5 Basic Flow).
Exceptions:
E1. Empty subject.
System detects a required field is empty.
System blocks submission and shows an inline validation message.
The actor completes the field and retries the action.
Priority:
Medium
Frequency of Use:
Low
2.2 Authentication
2.2.1 Register Account
UC ID and Name:
UC-01 Register Account
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
03/06/2026
Primary Actor:
Guest
Secondary Actors:
Email Service
Description:
As a Guest, I want to register a new account in the system so that I can access its authenticated and personalized features.
Trigger:
The Guest opens the registration page (/register) and clicks the “Đăng ký” (Register) button.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Guest is not logged in.
PRE-2. The Guest has a valid email address that is not already registered in the system.
Postconditions:
POST-1. A new user account is created in the database with the default Learner role.
POST-2. The email is verified through a valid OTP and the account is activated.
POST-3. The Guest is authenticated (access and refresh tokens issued) and redirected into the system.
Normal Flow:
1. The Guest opens the registration page.
2. The Guest enters Full name, Email, Password, and Confirm password.
3. The Guest clicks “Đăng ký” (Register).
4. System validates the password format (BR-01) and confirms the email is unique (BR-02).
5. System creates the account and sends a verification OTP to the registered email.
6. System opens the “Xác thực email” (Email verification) screen.
7. The Guest enters the OTP and clicks “Xác thực email” (Verify email).
8. System verifies the OTP (BR-06), activates the account, logs the Guest in, and displays MSG-03.
Alternative Flows:
4.A1 Invalid password formatSystem detects the password does not meet BR-01 and displays MSG-01.The Guest re-enters the password and continues from step 3.7.A1 Resend OTPThe Guest clicks “Gửi lại mã xác thực” (Resend code); System sends a new OTP and restarts the countdown.5.A1 Re-register unverified accountSystem detects the email is registered but not verified. System updates the account password and sends a new verification OTP instead of rejecting the registration.
Exceptions:
5.E1 Email Already Exists
System detects the email is already registered (BR-02).
System displays MSG-02.
8.E2 Wrong OTP
System detects the OTP is wrong (BR-06).
System displays MSG-13.
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
High
2.2.2 Log In
UC ID and Name:
UC-06 Log In
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
03/06/2026
Primary Actor:
System user
Secondary Actors:
None
Description:
As a user, I want to be able to log into the system so that I can access my personalized account and authenticated features.
Trigger:
The user opens the login page (/login), enters credentials, and clicks the “Đăng nhập” (Log in) button.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The user has a registered, activated account.
PRE-2. The user is not currently logged in.
Postconditions:
POST-1. The user is authenticated and an access/refresh token session is created.
POST-2. The failed-login attempt counter is reset to zero.
POST-3. The user is redirected to the home page or the page they originally requested.
Normal Flow:
1. System displays the login form.
2. The user enters Email and Password and clicks “Đăng nhập” (Log in).
3. System validates the credentials against the database (BR-01, BR-02).
4. System resets the failed-login attempt counter.
5. System creates the session and redirects the user to the home page (or the previously requested page).
Alternative Flows:
2.A1 Login with Google
The user clicks the “Google” button; System redirects to Google authentication.
The user authenticates with Google; Google returns a secure token and email.
System verifies the email exists in the database, logs the user in, and continues from step 5.
3.A2 Invalid credentials
System detects an incorrect email or password, increments the failed-login counter, and displays MSG-05.
The user re-enters credentials and continues from step 2.
5.A3 Unregistered Google email
System detects the Google email does not exist in the database, aborts login, and displays MSG-07.
Exceptions:
2.E1 Account LockedSystem detects the failed login counter has reached 5 (BR-03).System locks the account and prevents login.System displays MSG-06.4.E2 Google Authentication FailedSystem fails to communicate with Google API or user cancels the prompt.System displays MSG-14.2.E3 Unverified Email AttemptSystem detects the user account exists but email is not verified. System throws a RuntimeException blocking the login and displays a message prompting the user to verify their email.
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
Very High
2.2.3 Reset Password
UC ID and Name:
UC-07 Reset Password
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
03/06/2026
Primary Actor:
System user
Secondary Actors:
Email Service
Description:
As a user, I want to recover or reset my password when I forget it so that I can regain access to my account.
Trigger:
The user clicks the “Quên mật khẩu?” (Forgot password?) link on the login page.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The user is not logged in.
PRE-2. The email entered belongs to an existing, activated account.
Postconditions:
POST-1. A password-reset OTP is generated and sent to the user's registered email.
POST-2. On success, the user's password is updated in the database and the user can log in with the new password.
Normal Flow:
1. System displays the email input form.
2. The user enters their registered email and clicks “Gửi mã OTP đặt lại mật khẩu” (Send reset OTP).
3. System verifies the email exists in the database.
4. System generates an OTP with a 5-minute expiration and emails it to the user.
5. The user enters the OTP on the “Đặt lại mật khẩu” (Reset password) screen.
6. System verifies the OTP (BR-06).
7. The user enters New password and Confirm password and clicks “Lưu mật khẩu mới” (Save new password).
8. System validates the password format (BR-01) and updates the user's password in the database.
9. System displays MSG-03 and redirects the user to the login page. .
Alternative Flows:
3.A1 Email not foundSystem detects the email does not exist in the database and displays MSG-07.6.A1 Resend OTPThe user clicks “Gửi lại OTP” (Resend OTP); System sends a new OTP and restarts the countdown.8.A1 Invalid password formatSystem detects the password does not meet BR-01 and displays MSG-01; the user re-enters the password and continues from step 7.2.A1 Forgotten password - Email not foundSystem does not find the email but returns a generic success message silently to prevent email enumeration attacks.
Exceptions:
None
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
Medium
2.3 Learning Experience features
2.3.1 Add Course to Wishlist
UC ID and Name:
UC-15 Add Course to Wishlist
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
16/07/2026
Primary Actor:
Learner
Secondary Actors:
None
Description:
As a Learner, I want to add course to wishlist so that I can save an online course for later.
Trigger:
The Learner clicks the heart icon “Thêm vào danh sách yêu thích” (Add to wishlist) on a course,.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Learner is logged into the system.
PRE-2. The Learner must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The target course is published/active and publicly visible (BR-21).
PRE-4. The Learner is not already enrolled in the course (BR-20).
PRE-5. The course is not already saved in the wishlist (BR-20).
Postconditions:
POST-1. The course is added to the Learner's wishlist.
POST-2. The wishlist is persisted for the Learner across sessions.
Normal Flow:
1. The Learner opens a course from the catalog or course detail page and clicks the “Thêm vào danh sách yêu thích” (Add to wishlist) icon.
2. System verifies the Learner is logged in and that the course is not already in the wishlist.
3. System adds the course to the wishlist and persists it.
4. System displays MSG-28.
Alternative Flows:
2.A1 Course already in wishlist
1. System detects the course is already saved, does not create a duplicate, and displays MSG-30.
Exceptions:
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
*.E2 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.
3.E1 Course unavailable or already owned
1. System detects the course is unpublished, deleted, or the Learner is already enrolled.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and blocks the action.
3. System displays an error message.
3.E2 Course already in list
1. System detects the course is already in the cart or wishlist.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and blocks the addition.
3. System displays an error message.
Priority:
Medium
Frequency of Use:
Medium

Exceptions:
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
*.E2 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.
3.E1 Course unavailable or already owned
1. System detects the course is unpublished, deleted, or the Learner is already enrolled.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and blocks the action.
3. System displays an error message.
3.E2 Course already in list
1. System detects the course is already in the cart or wishlist.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and blocks the addition.
3. System displays an error message.
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
Medium
2.3.8 Pay for Courses in Cart
UC ID and Name:
UC-22 Pay for Courses in Cart
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
16/07/2026
Primary Actor:
Learner
Secondary Actors:
Payment Service (PayOS), Email Service
Description:
As a Learner, I want to pay for courses in cart so that I can start learning.
Trigger:
The Learner clicks “Mua khóa học” (Buy course) on a course detail page or “Tiếp tục thanh toán” (Continue to payment) in the cart.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Learner is logged into the system.
PRE-2. The Learner must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The cart contains at least one course.
PRE-4. Courses in the cart are still active/available and not already owned (BR-05, BR-21).
Postconditions:
POST-1. On success, the Learner is enrolled in all purchased courses and they appear in “Khóa học của tôi” (My Courses).
POST-2. A payment transaction record is created; for paid orders a confirmation/receipt email is sent.
POST-3. The purchased courses are removed from the cart.
Normal Flow:
1. The Learner clicks “Tiếp tục thanh toán” (Continue to payment) from the cart.
2. System displays the Checkout screen: the selected courses (thumbnail, title, target outcome, number of lessons) and an order summary (prices and total payable).
3. The Learner reviews the order and may enter a coupon and click “Áp dụng” (Apply).
4. The Learner selects a payment method (“Thanh toán qua PayOS”) and clicks “Xác nhận thanh toán” (Confirm payment).
5. System re-validates the cart: courses are still active, the Learner is not already enrolled, and the total is recomputed.
6. System initiates the transaction with the PayOS gateway and waits for the payment result.
7. On payment success, System enrolls the Learner in all courses, creates a payment record, and removes the purchased courses from the cart.
8. System sends a confirmation/receipt email and displays MSG-37.
9. System redirects the Learner to “Khóa học của tôi” (My Courses).
Alternative Flows:
4.A1 Free order (total = 0)
1. System shows the total as “Miễn phí” and the method/payment-info steps are skipped; the Learner clicks “Xác nhận ghi danh” (Confirm enrollment).
2. On confirmation, System enrolls the Learner immediately (continue at step 7) without invoking the PayOS gateway.
4.A2 Back to cart
1. The Learner clicks “Quay lại giỏ hàng” (Back to cart); System returns to the cart with no changes.
6.A1 Payment cancelled at gateway
1. The Learner cancels payment at PayOS; System returns to the Checkout screen, the order is not completed, and no enrollment is created.
Exceptions:
6.E1 Course unavailable or already enrolled
1. System removes the invalid course from the order, displays MSG-36, and asks the learner to review the order before paying.
7.E1 Payment failed or declined
1. PayOS returns a failure result; system displays MSG-38, no enrollment is created, and the cart is preserved.
7.E2 Payment gateway timeout / unreachable
1. System displays MSG-39, marks the transaction as failed/pending, and does not grant enrollment until the payment is confirmed.
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System redirects the learner to the Login screen.
4.E1 Invalid Cart Combination
1. System detects cart mixes online courses and class tuitions, or contains multiple class tuitions.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and blocks payment creation.
4.E2 Discount Code Invalid
1. System validates the applied discount code and finds it expired, inactive, or maxed out.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and aborts payment.
4.E3 Already Paid
1. System detects the Learner has already paid for the class tuition or online course.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and aborts payment.
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
Medium

Date Created:

As a Learner, I want to join free online course so that I can enroll in a free course without payment.

Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Learner is logged into the system.
PRE-2. The Learner must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The target course is published and priced at zero (free).
PRE-4. The Learner is not already enrolled in the target course (BR-05).
Postconditions:
POST-1. An enrollment record is created and the Learner is granted access to course content.
POST-2. The course appears in the Learner's My Courses screen.
Normal Flow:
1. The Learner opens a free course detail page.
2. The Learner clicks "Đăng ký miễn phí" (Enroll for free).

4. System creates the enrollment record without invoking the payment gateway.
5. System grants access to course content and displays MSG-11.
6. System redirects the Learner to My Courses.
Alternative Flows:
3.A1 Free checkout from cart
1. System shows the total as “Miễn phí” and the method/payment-info steps are skipped; the Learner clicks “Xác nhận ghi danh” (Confirm enrollment).

Exceptions:
2.E1 Enrollment Validation Failed
1. System detects that the course is inactive or the learner is already enrolled in the course (BR-05).
2. System blocks the enrollment process.
3. System displays MSG-10.
4.E1 Payment Failed
1. Payment gateway returns a failed, canceled, or expired transaction status.
2. System does not create the enrollment record.
3. System does not grant access to the course content.
4. System displays an appropriate payment failure message and allows the learner to retry payment.

Date Created:
16/07/2026

Secondary Actors:
None
Description:
As a Learner, I want to mark lesson as complete so that I can finish a lesson so the next one can open.
Trigger:
In the course workspace, the Learner clicks "Đánh dấu hoàn thành" (Mark complete).
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Learner is logged into the system.
PRE-2. The Learner must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The Learner is enrolled in the online (self-paced) course.
PRE-4. The selected lesson is unlocked (previous lesson completed for sequential courses) (BR-57).
PRE-5. The Learner has opened the lesson content.
Postconditions:
POST-1. Lesson progress is saved as COMPLETED.
POST-2. The next lesson is unlocked when the course is sequential (BR-57).
Normal Flow:
1. The Learner finishes studying the open lesson content.
2. The Learner clicks "Đánh dấu hoàn thành" (Mark complete).
3. System validates the lesson is unlocked and in progress (BR-57).
4. System saves lesson progress as COMPLETED.
5. System unlocks the next lesson when the course is sequential.
6. System updates the navigation panel to reflect the new status.
Alternative Flows:
5.A1 Last lesson in module — module test required
1. The Learner marks the last lesson in the module complete.
2. System shows the module test placed at the end of the module (BR-58).
3. The Learner opens and completes the module test via UC-29.
5.A2 Module test passed — unlock next module
1. System scores the module test attempt.
2. The Learner scores at least (course target score − 0.5) (BR-58).
3. System unlocks the first lesson of the next module and subsequent lessons per BR-57.
5.A3 Module test below threshold
1. System scores the module test below (course target score − 0.5) (BR-58).
2. The next module and its lessons remain locked.
3. The Learner must retake the module test before progressing.
Exceptions:
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
*.E2 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.

2.3.15 Complete Online Course Practice
UC ID and Name:
UC-29 Complete Online Course Practice
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
16/07/2026
Primary Actor:
Learner
Secondary Actors:
AI Engine Service
Description:
As a Learner, I want to complete online course practice so that I can finish one online course practice exercise and submit my answers.
Trigger:
The Learner opens a practice exercise and clicks "Vào chế độ làm bài" (Enter practice mode).
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Learner is logged into the system.
PRE-2. The Learner must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The Learner is enrolled in the online course.
PRE-4. The practice exercise is available and not yet passed (when module gate applies) (BR-58).
Postconditions:
POST-1. The Learner's answers or submission are saved and scored where applicable.
POST-2. Attempt results are recorded; module progression updates when the passing threshold is met (BR-58).
Normal Flow:
1. The Learner opens a practice exercise and enters practice mode.
2. System loads exercise content (prompt, audio player, or question parts) and starts the countdown timer when configured.
3. The Learner completes the exercise (types a response, selects answers, or records speaking under BR-09).
4. The Learner clicks "Nộp bài" (Submit) or the timer auto-submits (BR-08).
5. System evaluates the submission against the answer key or scoring rules.
6. System saves the attempt, displays the score/feedback, and when the exercise is a module test, unlocks the next module only if the score is at least (course target score − 0.5) (BR-58).
Alternative Flows:
4.A1 Exit before submitting
1. The Learner exits exam mode; System keeps the auto-saved draft so the Learner can resume later.
3.A1 Timed exercise — timer expires
1. System detects the countdown reaches zero (BR-08), auto-submits the saved answers, and displays MSG-16.
3.A2 Speaking exercise — time limit reached
1. System detects the recording reaches the 5-minute limit (BR-09) and automatically stops it; the Learner continues from step 4.
2.A1 Module test attempt
1. The Learner opens the module test (final practice at the end of the module).
2. System loads the module test and shows the course target score and pass rule (target − 0.5) (BR-58).
3. The Learner completes and submits the module test.
6.A1 Module test below pass threshold
1. System scores the module test below (course target score − 0.5) (BR-58).
2. The next module and its lessons remain locked.
3. The Learner must retake the module test before progressing.
6.A2 Module test passed — unlock next module
1. System scores the module test at or above (course target score − 0.5) (BR-58).
2. System unlocks the next module and its lessons (BR-57).
3. System displays the pass result and updated progression.
Exceptions:
4.E1 Timer expires
1. System detects the countdown reaches zero (BR-08), auto-submits the saved answers, and displays MSG-16.
3.E1 Speaking recording limit
1. System detects the recording reaches the 5-minute limit (BR-09) and automatically stops it; the Learner continues from step 4.
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
*.E2 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
High

1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
*.E2 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.
5.E1 Save failed
1. System fails to save the note due to a server or connection error.
2. System displays an error message.
3. System keeps the entered content in the editor so the Learner can retry.
4.E1 Invalid note content
1. System detects note content is empty or exceeds the allowed length (BR-48, BR-56).
2. System blocks save and shows inline validation.
3. The Learner corrects the content and retries.
Priority:
Medium
Frequency of Use:
Medium

2.3.21 Ask a Discussion Question
UC ID and Name:
UC-35 Ask a Discussion Question
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
16/07/2026
Primary Actor:
Learner
Secondary Actors:
None
Description:
As a Learner, I want to ask a discussion question so that I can post a new question.
Trigger:
In the Discussion tab, the Learner clicks "Đặt câu hỏi" (Ask question) and submits a new thread.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Learner is logged into the system.
PRE-2. The Learner must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The Learner is enrolled in the online course with ACTIVE or COMPLETED status (BR-36).
PRE-4. The target discussion thread or reply is visible and not HIDDEN (BR-40).
Postconditions:
POST-1. A new discussion thread is saved and visible to enrolled learners.
POST-2. Content matching prohibited phrases is stored as PENDING_REVIEW (BR-39).
Normal Flow:
1. The Learner opens the Discussion tab of an enrolled course.
2. The Learner clicks "Đặt câu hỏi" (Ask question).
3. The Learner enters a title (8–180 characters) and question body (20–3000 characters) (BR-35).
4. The Learner clicks "Gửi" (Send).
5. System validates enrollment (BR-36) and content rules (BR-35, BR-39).
6. System saves the thread and refreshes the discussion list.
Alternative Flows:
4.A1 Cancel compose
1. The Learner closes the compose form without sending.
2. System discards unsaved input; no thread is created.
Exceptions:
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
*.E2 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.
4.E1 Empty or invalid content
1. System detects the discussion title or body fails validation (BR-35).
2. System blocks submission and shows inline validation messages.
3. The Learner corrects the content and retries.
5.E1 Prohibited content pending review
1. System detects content matching configured prohibited phrases (BR-39).
2. System stores the post with PENDING_REVIEW status and masks it from public view.
3. System informs the Learner that the content is awaiting moderation.
Priority:
Medium
Frequency of Use:

5. System validates enrollment (BR-43) and duplicate report rules (BR-41).
6. System saves the report; at three reports the thread moves to PENDING_REVIEW unless already RESOLVED (BR-44).
Alternative Flows:

1. The Learner cancels the report form.
2. System closes the form without creating a report.

1. System detects the discussion title or body fails validation (BR-35).
2. System blocks submission and shows inline validation messages.
3. The Learner corrects the content and retries.
5.E1 Prohibited content pending review
1. System detects content matching configured prohibited phrases (BR-39).
2. System stores the post with PENDING_REVIEW status and masks it from public view.
3. System informs the Learner that the content is awaiting moderation.
5.E2 Already reported
1. System detects the Learner already reported this thread (BR-41).
2. System blocks duplicate submission and displays a message.

POST-1. The timetable for the selected period is displayed with the Learner's class sessions.
POST-2: The database not change.
Normal Flow:

3. System displays the timetable, marking each session with its date, time, mode (offline/virtual), and status.
4. The Learner clicks “Chi tiết” (Details) on a session to view more information.
Alternative Flows:
2.A1 No scheduled classes
1. System finds no sessions for the selected period and displays MSG-12.
Exceptions:
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
*.E2 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.

2.3.37 Submit Homework
UC ID and Name:
UC-51 Submit Homework
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
16/07/2026
Primary Actor:
Learner
Secondary Actors:
Email Service
Description:
As a Learner, I want to submit homework so that I can send finished homework to my teacher.
Trigger:
In the classroom “Bài tập” (Homework) tab, the Learner clicks “Làm bài & Nộp bài” (Do & Submit) and then “Nộp bài tập ngay” (Submit homework now).
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The learner must be successfully logged in and have a valid account in the system.
PRE-2. The learner must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The assignment is open and its deadline has not passed.
Postconditions:
POST-1. The homework file is saved to the database and the submission status is updated to “Submitted”.
POST-2. The submission becomes available to the teacher for grading (UC-72).
Normal Flow:
1. The Learner opens the assignment and clicks “Làm bài & Nộp bài” (Do & Submit).
2. The Learner selects a file from their device in the “Nộp bài tập” (Submit homework) drawer.
3. System validates the file format and size (BR-07).
4. The Learner clicks “Nộp bài tập ngay” (Submit homework now).
5. System saves the file, updates the status to “Submitted”, and displays MSG-03.
Alternative Flows:
3.A1 Invalid file upload
1. System detects the file exceeds the size limit or is not an allowed format (BR-07), blocks the upload, and displays MSG-15; the Learner re-selects a valid file.
5.A1 Resubmit
1. Before the deadline, the Learner clicks “Nộp lại bài” (Resubmit) to replace the previous submission; System updates the stored file.
Exceptions:
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
*.E2 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.
3.E1 Not Enrolled
1. System detects the Learner is not enrolled in the target classroom.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and blocks submission.
3.E2 Homework Not Open or Overdue
1. System detects the homework is not open yet or the deadline has passed.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and blocks submission.
3.E3 Already Graded
1. System detects the homework has already been graded by the Teacher.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and prevents resubmission.
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
High

Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Learner is logged into the system.
PRE-2. The Learner must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The Learner has at least one prior attempt on the selected exercise.
Postconditions:
POST-1. Past attempts for the Learner are listed with time, score, and status (BR-63).
POST-2. No data is modified (read-only use case).
Normal Flow:
1. The Learner opens a practice exercise they have attempted before.
2. The Learner clicks "Lịch sử làm bài" (Attempt history).
3. System retrieves attempts scoped to the authenticated Learner only (BR-63).
4. System displays each attempt with submission time, score, and evaluation status.
Alternative Flows:
3.A1 No prior attempts
1. System finds no attempts for this exercise.
2. System displays an empty-state message.
Exceptions:
*.E1 Not authenticated / session expired
1. System detects the session is invalid or expired.
2. System blocks the action.
3. System redirects the Learner to the Login screen.
*.E2 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.
3.E1 Unauthorized access
1. System detects an attempt to view another Learner's history (BR-63).
2. System blocks access and returns an error.
Priority:
Low
Frequency of Use:
Medium

The system displays MSG-04.
The use case ends.

The Content Manager enters search/filter criteria.
The system finds no matching data.
The system displays MSG-04.

2.4.12 Update Placement Test Settings
UC ID and Name:
UC-112 Update Placement Test Settings
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
29/06/2026
Primary Actor:
Content Manager
Secondary Actors:
None
Description:
As a Content Manager, I want to edit a placement exam or its questions so that the placement exam bank remains accurate, updated, and suitable for learner assessment.
Trigger:
The Content Manager selects the edit function for an existing placement exam/question.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Content Manager is logged in.
PRE-2. The Content Manager has permission to update placement exam content.
PRE-3. The target placement exam/question exists in the system.
PRE-4. The current data of the target exam/question has been loaded for editing.
PRE-5. The edited question must still belong to a valid skill section.
PRE-6. Required fields must remain complete after editing.
PRE-7. For objective questions, the edited answer options and correct answer must remain valid.
PRE-8. For Writing and Speaking questions, the edited prompt/task instruction must remain clear and non-empty.
PRE-9. The edited score value must be a valid positive number.
PRE-10. If the exam/question has already been used in learner attempts, the update must not damage or invalidate existing learner result records.
Postconditions:
POST-1. The selected placement exam/question is updated successfully.
POST-2. The updated content is saved in the database.
POST-3. The updated data replaces the previous version for future use.
POST-4. Related answer keys and score values are updated according to the edited content.
POST-5. The last updated date/time of the exam/question is changed.
POST-6. The updated exam/question can be viewed by authorized Content Managers.
POST-7. If the updated exam/question is active, learners will use the updated version in future attempts.
POST-8. Existing learner attempt records remain preserved.
POST-9. If validation fails, the previous valid version remains unchanged.
Normal Flow:
1. The Content Manager selects an existing placement exam/question.
2. The Content Manager chooses the edit function.
3. The system displays the current exam/question information.
4. The Content Manager modifies the necessary fields.
5. The Content Manager updates answer options and correct answers where applicable.
6. The Content Manager updates the score value if needed.
7. The Content Manager saves the changes.
8. The system validates the updated data.
9. The system saves the updated exam/question.
10. The system displays MSG-03.
Alternative Flows:
4.A1 Content Manager cancels editing
The Content Manager cancels the edit action.
The system discards unsaved changes.
The original exam/question data remains unchanged.
Exceptions:
? EX-1. Target item not found:
The system displays an error message if the selected exam/question does not exist.
EX-2. Missing required information:
The system displays a validation message and prevents saving.
EX-3. Invalid answer configuration:
The system displays an error if the answer options or correct answers are invalid.
EX-4. Invalid score value:
The system displays an error if the score value is not valid.
EX-5. Update restricted due to existing attempts:
If the update may affect existing learner results, the system prevents the update or requires the item to be duplicated/archived according to business rules.
Priority:
Medium
Frequency of Use:
Medium

16/07/2026
Primary Actor:
Teacher
Secondary Actors:
None
Description:
As a Teacher, I want to record student attendance so that I can mark students present or absent.
Trigger:
On the attendance checklist, the Teacher sets learner statuses and clicks “Lưu bảng điểm danh” (Save attendance) when no attendance has been saved for the session.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Teacher is logged in.
PRE-2. The Teacher must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The Teacher is assigned to the classroom (BR-11).
PRE-4. No attendance record exists yet for the selected session.
Postconditions:
POST-1. A new attendance record for the session is saved in the database.
Normal Flow:
1. The Teacher selects a past or current session and opens the attendance checklist.
2. System displays the roster of enrolled learners.
3. The Teacher sets each learner's status: “Có mặt” (Present), “Vắng mặt” (Absent), “Đi muộn” (Late), or “Có phép” (Excused).
4. The Teacher clicks “Lưu bảng điểm danh” (Save attendance).
5. System validates the date (BR-12), creates the attendance record, and displays MSG-03.
Alternative Flows:
2.A1 Batch mark all as present1. The Teacher clicks “Tất cả có mặt” (Mark All Present).2. System sets attendance status to PRESENT for all enrolled learners in the roster.3. The Teacher adjusts any absent or late learners before saving.2.A2 Record excused absence with note1. The Teacher selects EXCUSED for a student with permitted absence and enters the excuse reason into the note field.2. System saves the reason alongside the attendance record.
Exceptions:
5.E1 Future Date Selected: System detects the session date is in the future (BR-12), blocks saving, and displays MSG-20.
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
High

2.5.10 Grade Homework Submission
UC ID and Name:
UC-72 Grade Homework Submission
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
16/07/2026
Primary Actor:
Teacher
Secondary Actors:
AI Engine Service, Email Service
Description:
As a Teacher, I want to grade homework submission so that I can score and give feedback.
Trigger:
On a submitted homework item, the Teacher enters a score and feedback and clicks “Chấm điểm” (Grade).
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Teacher is logged in.
PRE-2. The Teacher must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The Teacher is assigned to the classroom (BR-11).
PRE-4. The target learner submission exists and is in a gradable state.
Postconditions:
POST-1. The submission is marked graded with the entered score and feedback.
POST-2. The learner can view the grade when the gradebook is published (BR-12).
Normal Flow:
1. The Teacher opens a learner submission from the homework submissions view.
2. System displays the submitted work and any rubric fields.
3. The Teacher enters Score and Feedback.
4. The Teacher clicks “Chấm điểm” (Grade) or “Cập nhật điểm” (Update score).
5. System validates the score range, saves the grade, and displays MSG-03.
Alternative Flows:
2.A1 AI-assisted pre-grading suggestion1. The Teacher clicks “Gợi ý chấm bằng AI” (AI Evaluation).2. System calls AI Engine Service to analyze the submission, generating rubric breakdown scores and feedback draft suggestions.3. The Teacher reviews, adjusts the scores or comments, and proceeds to confirm the final grade.2.A2 Request resubmission1. The Teacher selects “Yêu cầu nộp lại” (Request Resubmission) and enters feedback explaining required corrections.2. System resets the submission state to allow the learner to re-upload before the revised deadline.
Exceptions:
*.E1 Session expired / not authenticated
1. System detects an invalid or expired JWT.
2. System blocks the action and redirects the Teacher to the Login screen.
*.E2 Unauthorized classroom access
1. System detects the Teacher is not assigned to the target classroom (BR-11).
2. System blocks the action and displays MSG-19.
*.E3 System or network error
1. System cannot complete the request because of a server or network failure.
2. System displays a generic error message.
3. No data is changed unless the operation already succeeded.
5.E1 Submission not gradable
1. System detects the submission is not in a valid state for grading.
2. System throws a RuntimeException and aborts grading.
5.E2 Invalid score
1. System detects the score is outside the allowed range.
2. System blocks save and shows inline validation.
3. The Teacher corrects the score and retries.
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
High

As a Staff, I want to review and assess teaching quality and metrics so that I can evaluate teacher performance.
Trigger:
Within a teacher's profile, the Staff opens the “Đánh giá hiệu suất” (Evaluate Performance) tab.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Staff is logged in.
PRE-2. The Staff must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The selected teacher has accumulated teaching metrics to evaluate.
Postconditions:
POST-1. The performance evaluation record is saved for the teacher.
Normal Flow:
1. System aggregates and displays the teacher's metrics (e.g., learner feedback scores, attendance rates).
2. The Staff reviews the metrics.
3. The Staff enters qualitative feedback and a performance rating.
4. The Staff clicks “Lưu đánh giá” (Save evaluation).
5. System saves the evaluation and displays MSG-03.
Alternative Flows:
2.A1 Filter by campus and course program1. The Staff selects a specific Campus, Curriculum Program, or Date Range filter.2. System recalculates registration demand metrics and updates chart visualizations accordingly.2.A2 Export demand report data1. The Staff clicks “Xuất báo cáo” (Export Data).2. System compiles the filtered demand statistics into an Excel/CSV file and downloads it to the device.
Exceptions:
E1. Invalid/Expired JWT Token
System detects the JWT is invalid or expired.
System blocks the current action.
System redirects the Staff to the Login page.
Priority:
Medium
Frequency of Use:
Low (Monthly/Quarterly)

2.6.3 View Online Course Revenue Report
UC ID and Name:
UC-120 View Online Course Revenue Report
Created By:
ChinhCD
Date Created:
03/06/2026
Primary Actor:
Manager
Secondary Actors:
None
Description:
As a Manager, I want to track and analyze the financial performance and sales of courses so that I can monitor revenue.
Trigger:
The Manager opens the “Phân tích doanh thu” (Revenue Analytics) dashboard for online courses.
Preconditions:
PRE-1. The Manager is logged into the system.
PRE-2. The Manager must have a valid JWT token.
PRE-3. The Manager is authorized to view financial/revenue reports.
PRE-4. At least one online-course payment/enrollment transaction has been recorded in the system.
Postconditions:
POST-1. The requested revenue analytics (totals, breakdowns, and trends) are displayed as summary cards, charts, and tables.
POST-2. No financial data is modified (read-only use case).
POST-3. If the Manager chooses to export, the report file (e.g., Excel/PDF) is downloaded to the Manager's device.
Normal Flow:
1. The Manager opens the “Phân tích doanh thu” (Revenue Analytics) dashboard.
2. The Manager selects a date range and optional filters (course category, a specific course, payment status).
3. System queries the online-course payment and enrollment records matching the selected criteria.
4. System calculates the key metrics: total revenue, number of successful transactions, average order value, and refund/cancellation rate.
5. System aggregates revenue by course and by category and computes the revenue trend over time.
6. System renders the results into summary cards, charts (revenue trend and revenue by category), and a detailed table of top-selling courses.
7. The Manager reviews the analytics and may drill down for more detail.
Alternative Flows:
2.A1 Compare periods
The Manager enables period comparison (e.g., this month vs last month); System overlays both periods on the charts.
6.A1 Drill down by course
The Manager clicks a course in the table; System shows the detailed transaction list and revenue breakdown for that course.
7.A1 Export report
The Manager clicks “Xuất báo cáo” (Export report); System generates an Excel/PDF file and downloads it to the device.
Exceptions:
3.E1 No data
System finds no transaction data for the selected period/filters and displays MSG-04.
3.E2 Data load error
System fails to retrieve the financial data; System displays a generic error and keeps the previous view unchanged.
Priority:
High
Frequency of Use:
High

3. Functional Requirements
3.1 Authentication
3.1.1 User Login
Related Use Case(s): UC-06 – Log In
The Login Screen allows an existing user to authenticate using email and password.
The system validates:
-       Required fields (no empty values)
-                  Email format
-                  Credentials correctness
On successful login, the system returns an AuthResponse containing token, expiration time, email verification status, and user information.
Field Name
Description
email
String, required, must be a valid email format.
password
String, required.
rememberMe
Boolean.
Element
Description
Login Button
Action: Submits credentials. Disabled until required fields valid.
Forgot Password
Action: Redirects to Forgot Password screen.
Google Login
Action: Initiates Google OAuth authentication flow.
Register Link
Action: Redirects to Register screen.
3.1.2 User Login with Google
Related Use Case(s): UC-06 – Log In
The system supports authentication via Google OAuth 2.0.
The user clicks “Login with Google”, authenticates with their Google account, and the system receives a Google ID token. The system verifies the token and either:
-               Creates a new account (if the Google email is not registered) and returns an AuthResponse.
-               Logs in the existing account (if the Google email is already registered) and returns an AuthResponse.
3.1.3 User Register
Related Use Case(s): UC-01 – Register Account
Step 1 – Submit registration form. On success, an OTP is sent to the user’s email and an OtpVerificationResponse is returned containing a verification token.
Field Name
Description
name
String, required, valid name format.
email
String, required, valid email format.
password
String, required, 8–100 chars.
confirmPassword
String, required, 8–100 chars, match with password field.
Element
Description
Sign Up Button
Action: Submits registration form.
Login Link
Action: Redirects to Login screen.
Step 2 – User enters the OTP code received via email. The system verifies the OTP and returns an AuthResponse with access token and refresh token.
Field Name
Description
otp
String, required, 6 chars.
Element
Description
Verify Button
Action: Submits verification form.
Login Link
Action: Redirects to Login screen.
3.1.4 Forgot Password
Related Use Case(s): UC-07 – Reset Password
The Forgot Password flow allows a user to request a password reset link via email.
The system validates:
-                  Required field (email must not be empty)
-                  Email format
-                  Email must belong to an existing account
On success, a password reset token is generated and sent to the user’s email.
Field Name
Description
email
String, required, must be a valid email format.
Element
Description
Send Reset Link Button
Action: Submits email. Sends password reset link to email.
Back to Login Link
Action: Redirects to Login screen.
3.1.5 Reset Password
Related Use Case(s): UC-07 – Reset Password
The Reset Password Screen allows a user to set a new password after verifying the email in forgot password.
The system validates:
·    Token validity (not expired, not already used)
·    New password rules (8–100 characters)
On success, the password is updated and the user can log in with the new password.
Field Name
Description
newPassword
String, required, 8–100 chars.
confirmPassword
String, required, 8–100 chars, match with newPassword.
Element
Description
Reset Password Button
Action: Submits new password. Redirects to Login on success.
3.1.6 Email Verification
Related Use Case(s): UC-01 – Register Account
The system supports email verification to confirm the user’s email address.
-                  When registration, the user can request an email verification link.
-                  The system sends a verification token to the user’s email.
-                  The user clicks the link (or submits the token) to verify their email.
-                  On success, the email is marked as verified and an AuthResponse is returned.
Field Name
Description
email
String, required (for requesting verification). Valid email format.
token
String, required (for verifying). The verification token from the email.
Element
Description
Verify Email Link
Action: User clicks link in email to verify. Submits token.
3.1.7 Logout
Related Use Case(s): N/A – No corresponding use case is defined in the current Use Case Catalog.
The Logout action invalidates the user’s refresh token, effectively ending the session.
On success, the refresh token is revoked and the user must re-authenticate to access the system.
Field Name
Description
token
String, required. The token to invalidate.
Element
Description
Logout Button
Action: Sends token to server. Redirects to Login screen.
3.2 Learning Experience
3.2.1 Course Enrollment
3.2.1.1 Course Detail and Enrollment
Related Use Case(s): UC-03 – View Online Course Details; UC-18 – Add Course to Cart; UC-24 – Join Free Online Course
The Enroll Course screen is the public-facing course detail page. It allows any visitor to browse course information and allows authenticated learners to enroll in or purchase a course.
The screen provides:
●         Course header: title, price label, and enrollment button.
●         Course metadata bar: band level, rating, difficulty, total lessons, and total duration.
●        Tabbed info panels: Introduction (Giới thiệu), Results (Kết quả), Skills (Kỹ năng), Prerequisites (Điều Kiện).
●        Modules and lessons section: each module is an expandable accordion listing its lessons with duration and a Preview button for free-preview lessons.
Field Name
Description
courseTitle
String, read-only. The name of the course displayed at the top of the page (e.g., “IELTS Master Vocabulary Band 6.5”).
priceLabel
String, read-only. Displays the course price or “Miễn phí” (Free) if the course is free.
bandLevel
String, read-only. The IELTS band range the course targets (e.g., “Band 5.5 – 6.5”).
ratingLabel
String, read-only. Average learner rating or “Chưa có đánh giá” if no ratings exist.
difficulty
String, read-only. The difficulty level of the course (e.g., “Nâng cao”).
totalLessons
String, read-only. Total number of lessons in the course (e.g., “32 bài học”).
totalDuration
String, read-only. Estimated total study time (e.g., “8 giờ 9 phút”).
moduleName
String, read-only. The title of each module displayed in the accordion (e.g., “Module 1: Family and Relationship”).
lessonName
String, read-only. The title of each lesson within a module.
lessonDuration
String, read-only. The estimated study time per lesson (e.g., “10 phút”).
Element
Description
Buy Course Button (“Mua khóa học”)
Action: Initiates the enrollment or payment process. For free courses, enrolls the learner immediately. For paid courses, redirects to the payment flow.
Info Tabs (Giới thiệu / Kết quả / Kỹ năng / Điều Kiện)
Action: Switches the content panel between Introduction, Results, Skills, and Prerequisites sections.
Module Accordion
Toggle: Expands or collapses a module to show or hide its lesson list.
Preview Button
Action: Opens a lesson preview for free-preview lessons. Visible to both enrolled and unenrolled users.
Back to Course List Link (“Quay lại danh sách khóa học”)
Action: Navigates the user back to the online course catalog page.
3.2.1.2 Wishlist Courses
Related Use Case(s): UC-15 – Add Course to Wishlist; UC-16 – Remove Course from Wishlist; UC-17 – View My Wishlist; UC-21 – Move Course from Wishlist to Cart
The Wishlist Courses screen displays the list of courses the learner has marked as favorites for later reference.
The screen provides:
●        A shortcut to open the shopping cart.
●        For each saved course: a thumbnail, the course title, and a “Saved to wishlist” label.
●        Per-course actions to view the course, add it to the cart, or remove it from the wishlist.
Removing a course updates the list immediately. The wishlist is persisted across sessions for the logged-in learner.
Field Name
Description
courseTitle
String, read-only. The title of each saved course (e.g., “E2 IELTS Practise”).
wishlistLabel
String, read-only. A status label indicating the course is saved to the wishlist.
thumbnail
Image, read-only. The cover image of the course.
Element
Description
Open Cart Button
Action: Navigates to the shopping cart.
View Course Button
Action: Opens the course detail page of the selected course.
Add to Cart Button
Action: Adds the selected saved course to the shopping cart.
Remove from Wishlist Button
Action: Removes the selected course from the wishlist and refreshes the list.
3.2.1.3 Shopping Cart
Related Use Case(s): UC-18 – Add Course to Cart; UC-19 – Remove Course from Cart; UC-20 – View My Cart; UC-22 – Pay for Courses in Cart
The Shopping Cart screen lets a learner review the courses they have added before continuing to checkout. It shows each selected course with its thumbnail, title, and price, together with an order summary, and lets the learner remove a course, move it to the wishlist, or continue to payment.
The screen provides:
●        A cart header with a title and a shortcut to open the wishlist.
●        A list of cart items, each showing a thumbnail, course title, price, and per-course actions.
●        An order summary panel showing the number of courses and the total amount payable.
●        A control to continue to the checkout and payment step.
Field Name
Description
cartTitle
String, read-only. The title of the cart screen (e.g., “Giỏ hàng”).
cartItemThumbnail
Image, read-only. The thumbnail image of each course in the cart.
courseTitle
String, read-only. The title of each course in the cart (e.g., “E2 IELTS Practise”).
coursePrice
String, read-only. The price of each course, shown as “Miễn phí” when the course is free.
courseCount
String, read-only. The number of courses in the cart shown in the order summary (e.g., “Số khóa học: 2”).
orderSummaryLabel
String, read-only. The heading of the order summary panel (e.g., “Tóm tắt đơn hàng”).
totalPrice
String, read-only. The total amount payable for the cart (e.g., “Tổng thanh toán: Miễn phí”).
Element
Description
Open Wishlist Button (“Mở danh sách yêu thích”)
Action: Opens the learner’s wishlist.
View Course Button (“Xem khóa học”)
Action: Opens the detail page of the selected course.
Move to Wishlist Button (“Chuyển sang danh sách yêu thích”)
Action: Moves the course from the cart to the wishlist.
Remove Course Button (“Xóa khỏi giỏ hàng”)
Action: Removes the course from the cart and updates the order summary.
Continue to Payment Button (“Tiếp tục thanh toán”)
Action: Proceeds to the Checkout and Payment screen.
Order Summary Panel
Display: Shows the number of courses and the total amount payable.
3.2.1.4 Checkout and Payment
Related Use Case(s): UC-22 – Pay for Courses in Cart
The Checkout and Payment screen lets a learner review their selected courses and complete enrollment. It lists each course with its details and an order summary showing the total amount, then lets the learner complete the payment or return to the cart. For free courses the total is shown as “Miễn phí”; for paid courses the learner selects a payment method and enters the payment details.
The screen provides:
●        A checkout header with a title and a short instruction.
●        A list of the selected courses, each showing a thumbnail, title, target outcome, and number of lessons.
●        An order summary panel listing the courses, their prices, and the total amount payable.
●        Controls to complete the payment or return to the cart.
Field Name
Description
checkoutTitle
String, read-only. The title of the checkout screen (e.g., “Thanh toán”).
courseTitle
String, read-only. The title of each course in the order (e.g., “IELTS Master Vocabulary”).
courseOutcome
String, read-only. The target outcome of each course (e.g., “Mục tiêu đầu ra:”).
lessonCount
String, read-only. The number of lessons in each course (e.g., “Số bài học:”).
coursePrice
String, read-only. The price of each course, shown as “Miễn phí” when the course is free.
paymentMethod
String, required. The payment method selected for paid courses (e.g., bank transfer or QR code).
paymentInformation
String (text input), required. The payment details entered by the learner for paid courses.
orderSummaryLabel
String, read-only. The heading of the order summary panel (e.g., “Tóm tắt đơn hàng”).
totalAmount
String, read-only. The total amount payable for the order (e.g., “Tổng thanh toán: Miễn phí”).
Element
Description
Payment Method Selector
Action: Lets the learner choose a payment method for paid courses.
Payment Information Input
Action: Lets the learner enter the required payment details for paid courses.
Order Summary Panel
Display: Lists the selected courses, their prices, and the total amount payable.
Confirm Payment Button (“Tiếp tục thanh toán”)
Action: Confirms the order and completes the payment.
Back to Cart Button (“Quay lại giỏ hàng”)
Action: Returns to the shopping cart.

3.2.2 Online Course Learning
3.2.2.1 Access Lessons
Related Use Case(s): UC-26 – Access Online Course Lessons; UC-27 – Mark Lesson as Complete
The Access Lessons screen is the main lesson viewer where an enrolled learner reads course content lesson by lesson. It provides a structured reading environment with a left navigation panel, a content area, and a notes sidebar.
The screen provides:
●        A progress bar at the top showing overall course completion percentage.
●        A left navigation panel listing all modules and their lessons, with locked/unlocked status indicated.
●        A main content area displaying the currently opened lesson, organized into named sections.
●        A right-side notes panel for the learner to write personal study notes.
●        Sequential lesson unlocking: a lesson must be marked complete before the next lesson is unlocked.
Field Name
Description
courseTitle
String, read-only. The title of the enrolled course displayed at the top of the left navigation panel.
completionProgress
String, read-only. Overall course completion percentage shown in the top progress bar (e.g., “0%”).
moduleName
String, read-only. The label of the module grouping lessons in the left panel and above the lesson content area (e.g., “Module 1”).
lessonName
String, read-only. The title of the currently opened lesson displayed in the content area (e.g., “Tên Bài học”).
lessonType
String, read-only. The category or type label of the lesson (e.g., “Loại bài”).
lessonDuration
String, read-only. The estimated time to complete the lesson (e.g., “Thời lượng”).
sectionTitle
String, read-only. The heading of each content section within the lesson body (e.g., “Tiêu đề phần”).
sectionContent
String, read-only. The body text or media content of each lesson section.
lessonStatus
String, read-only. Indicates whether a lesson in the left panel is accessible or locked (e.g., “Khóa” for locked lessons).
Element
Description
Back to Course Detail Link (“← Quay lại chi tiết khóa học”)
Action: Navigates the learner back to the course detail/enrollment page.
Study Mode Button (“Học theo bài”)
Action: Switches to lesson-by-lesson study mode. This is the default active mode.
Flashcard Mode Button (“Thẻ ghi nhớ”)
Action: Switches the view to the vocabulary flashcard interface for the current lesson.
Module Accordion (Left Panel)
Toggle: Expands or collapses a module in the left navigation panel to show or hide its lessons.
Lesson Navigation Item (Left Panel)
Action: Opens the selected unlocked lesson in the content area. Locked lessons are displayed as disabled and cannot be clicked.
Notes Panel (“Ghi chú”)
Display: A side panel where the learner can type and save personal study notes for the current lesson.
Previous Lesson Button (“Bài trước”)
Action: Navigates to the previous lesson in the module.
Complete-to-Unlock Hint (“Hoàn thành bài hiện tại để mở bài tiếp theo”)
Display: Hint that the current lesson must be completed to unlock the next one. The action itself is the Mark as Complete button.
Mark as Complete Button (“✓ Đánh dấu hoàn thành”)
Action: Marks the currently open lesson as completed and updates the learner’s overall course progress.
