3.2.3 Course Practice
3.2.3.1 Flashcard Practice
Related Use Case(s): UC-42 – Practice Flashcards in Online Course; UC-43 – Practice Flashcards in Classroom; UC-44 – Practice Flashcards from Flashcard Hub; UC-45 – Update Flashcard Progress
The Flashcard Practice screen provides an interactive vocabulary study experience for the learner. It is accessed from within a course lesson and presents vocabulary terms as digital flashcards that the learner can flip, rate, and organize by learning status. The screen also includes the course’s left navigation panel listing modules and lessons.
The screen provides:
●        Three study mode tabs: Card view (Thẻ), Study mode (Học), and Matching game (Ghép).
●        A flashcard display area showing the vocabulary term on the front; clicking the card flips it to reveal the definition.
●        Navigation controls to move between cards and rate each card as known, almost known, or not yet known.
●        A progress tracking toggle (Theo dõi tiến độ) that records study session progress.
●        A vocabulary status panel below the card, categorized into: “Đang học” (Learning), “Chưa học” (Not studied), and “Đã thuộc” (Mastered).
Field Name
Description
courseTitle
String, read-only. The title of the enrolled course displayed in the left navigation panel.
completionProgress
String, read-only. Overall course completion percentage displayed in the top progress bar (e.g., “0%”).
sectionLabel
String, read-only. Label indicating the vocabulary category for the current lesson (e.g., “TỪ VỰNG MỚI”).
screenTitle
String, read-only. The title of the flashcard screen (e.g., “Thẻ ghi nhớ từ vựng”).
flashcardTerm
String, read-only. The vocabulary term displayed on the front face of the flashcard (e.g., “Thuật ngữ”).
flashcardDefinition
String, read-only. The definition or explanation revealed when the card is flipped.
cardIndex
String, read-only. Current card position out of the total number of cards (e.g., “1 / N”).
learningStatusCount
String, read-only. Categorized vocabulary counts: “Đang học (0)”, “Chưa học (N)”, “Đã thuộc (0)”.
vocabularyItem
String, read-only. Each vocabulary term listed under the learning status sections (e.g., “Từ vựng 1”).
vocabularyModule
String, read-only. The module source label for each vocabulary item (e.g., “Mô-đun 1”).
Element
Description
Back to Course Detail Link (“← Quay lại chi tiết khóa học”)
Action: Navigates back to the course detail page.
Study Mode Button (“Học theo bài”)
Action: Switches to the lesson-by-lesson reading view.
Flashcard Mode Button (“Thẻ ghi nhớ”)
Action: Currently active. Displays the vocabulary flashcard practice interface.
Mode Tabs (Thẻ / Học / Ghép)
Action: Switches between flashcard display modes — Card view (Thẻ), Study mode (Học), and Matching game (Ghép).
Flashcard Card Area
Action: Displays the vocabulary term on the front. Click to flip the card and reveal the definition on the back.
Track Progress Toggle (“Theo dõi tiến độ”)
Action: Enables or disables progress tracking for the current flashcard session. When enabled, card ratings update the learning status lists.
Previous Card Button (←)
Action: Navigates to the previous flashcard in the deck.
Next Card Button (→)
Action: Navigates to the next flashcard in the deck.
Knowledge Rating Buttons (3 buttons)
Action: Rates the card as “Don’t know”, “Almost know”, or “Know”; the card then moves to the matching learning-status list.
Favourite / Star Icon
Action: Marks or unmarks a vocabulary item as a favourite for quick reference.
Audio Icon
Action: Plays the pronunciation audio for the vocabulary term.
Module Accordion (Left Panel)
Toggle: Expands or collapses modules in the left navigation panel.
Lesson Navigation Item (Left Panel)
Action: Opens the selected lesson. Locked lessons (“Khóa”) are shown as disabled.
3.2.3.2 Module Final Quiz
Related Use Case(s): UC-28 – View Practice in Online Course; UC-29 – Complete Online Course Practice
The Module Final Quiz screen is the end-of-module assessment, taken after the learner completes all lessons in the module. The learner composes sentences using a target vocabulary word, and an AI engine evaluates the answer for correctness, context, and naturalness.
The screen provides:
●        An exam mode header with a violation counter and an exit button.
●        A question area displaying the target vocabulary prompt and grading mode tags.
●        A text area for the learner to write their answer, with a real-time sentence and word counter.
●        A right-side panel showing completion level, per-criterion progress, a pre-submission checklist, and exam mode rules.
●        An exam integrity warning banner that monitors tab switching, copy/paste, and fullscreen exit events.
Field Name
Description
examTitle
String, read-only. The title of the quiz displayed in the header (e.g., “Kiểm tra đầu ra từ vựng”).
violationCount
Number, read-only. The number of exam integrity violations recorded during the session (e.g., “Vi phạm đã ghi nhận: 0”).
questionSection
String, read-only. Label of the question group section (e.g., “CÂU SỬ DỤNG TỪ VỰNG MỤC TIÊU”).
questionContent
String, read-only. The prompt or question text shown to the learner.
examTags
String, read-only. Badges indicating the grading mode (e.g., “Vocabulary output”, “AI checks meaning”).
answerInput
String (textarea), required. The learner’s written answer, with a live sentence and word count.
sentenceCount
Number, read-only. Real-time count of sentences in the learner’s answer (e.g., “0 câu”).
wordCount
Number, read-only. Real-time count of words in the learner’s answer (e.g., “0 từ”).
completionLevel
Number, read-only. The overall answer quality percentage calculated by the AI engine (e.g., “0%”).
completionCriteria
String, read-only. Breakdown of grading criteria with individual progress (e.g., “5–7 câu”, “Đủ ngữ cảnh”, “Tự nhiên”).
checklistItems
String, read-only. Pre-submission checklist displayed to remind the learner of answer quality requirements before submitting.
examModeDescription
String, read-only. The description of the active exam mode and its anti-cheating monitoring rules.
Element
Description
Exam Mode Tags (“Vocabulary output” / “AI checks meaning”)
Display: Read-only badges indicating the question type and the grading method applied to the answer.
Submit for Grading Button (“Gửi chấm từ vựng”)
Action: Submits the learner’s written answer to the AI grading engine for evaluation. Active only when the answer field is not empty.
Exit Exam Button (“Thoát bài thi”)
Action: Exits the exam session, with a confirmation prompt to prevent accidental loss of progress.
Exam Integrity Warning Banner
Display: An always-visible banner informing the learner that exam mode is active. The system records tab switching, fullscreen exit, page navigation, copy/paste, and navigation shortcut usage as violations.
3.2.3.3 Listening Practice
Related Use Case(s): UC-28 – View Practice in Online Course; UC-29 – Complete Online Course Practice
The Listening Practice screen presents a full IELTS-format listening assessment. The learner listens to an auto-playing audio track and answers questions across multiple parts in real time, under a countdown timer.
The screen provides:
●        An exam header with the test title, a countdown timer, audio playback status, violation counter, and Submit / Exit buttons.
●        A multi-part structure (Part 1–4), each containing a passage title, a listening context description, question groups with instructions, and answer input fields.
●        A bottom question navigation panel showing all question numbers color-coded by status (answered, flagged, unanswered) with part-level progress counts.
●        An audio source label identifying the active audio track.
●        Real-time tracking of answered questions and remaining time.
Field Name
Description
examTitle
String, read-only. The title of the listening practice test displayed in the exam header.
partLabel
String, read-only. The label of the currently selected listening part.
passageTitle
String, read-only. The title of the listening passage or scenario displayed in the content area.
progressInfo
String, read-only. The learner’s answered-question progress for the listening test.
audioSourceLabel
String, read-only. The label identifying the audio source used for the listening section.
countdownTimer
String, read-only. The remaining exam time displayed as a countdown timer.
violationCount
Number, read-only. The number of exam integrity violations recorded during the listening session.
questionGroupLabel
String, read-only. The label for a group of questions within the selected part.
instruction
String, read-only. The instruction text explaining how the learner should answer the question group.
answerInput
String (text input), required. The learner’s typed answer for each fill-in-the-blank question; saved automatically as the learner types.
Element
Description
Audio Status Indicator
Display: Shows that the listening audio is currently playing automatically. It is read-only and does not allow the learner to pause or rewind the audio.
Exit Button
Action: Exits the listening exam session with a confirmation prompt to prevent accidental loss of answers.
Submit Button
Action: Submits all listening answers and ends the exam. A confirmation prompt is shown before submission.
Part Navigation Panel
Action: Switches the main content area to the selected listening part and shows progress for each part.
Flag Question Button
Action: Marks or unmarks the current question for later review. Flagged questions are highlighted in the question navigation panel
Question Number Dots
Display: Shows visual indicators for all question numbers and their current answer status.
Answer Input Field
Action: Allows the learner to type an answer for a fill-in-the-blank question. The answer is saved automatically while typing.
3.2.3.4 Writing Practice
Related Use Case(s): UC-28 – View Practice in Online Course; UC-29 – Complete Online Course Practice
The Writing Practice screen presents a full IELTS-format writing assessment. An enrolled learner reads each task prompt, composes their answer in a dedicated answer area, and moves between tasks, all under a single countdown timer and exam-mode monitoring. The learner can view the task instructions and any accompanying image, type and edit their answer, track the word count against the task target, and submit the whole Writing section when finished.
The screen provides:
●        An exam header with the test title, a countdown timer, a violation counter, and Finish Writing / Exit buttons.
●        A task prompt area (left) showing the task label, title, word-count guidance, the instructions, and any image provided for the task.
●        An answer area (right) with a text area for the response, a live word count, the task’s word target, and the total word count across the exam.
●        A bottom task navigation bar listing each task (Task 1, Task 2) with its word count, plus previous and next task controls.
Field Name
Description
examTitle
String, read-only. The full name of the writing practice test (e.g., “IELTS Placement Test 2025 January Writing Practice Test 1”).
countdownTimer
String, read-only. The remaining exam time displayed as a MM:SS countdown (e.g., “59:00”).
violationCount
Number, read-only. The number of recorded exam integrity violations (e.g., “Vi phạm: 0”).
taskLabel
String, read-only. The label of the current task section (e.g., “PART 1”).
taskTitle
String, read-only. The heading of the current writing task (e.g., “Writing Task 1”).
taskGuidance
String, read-only. The word-count guidance for the task (e.g., “Gợi ý : Tối thiểu 150 từ”).
taskPrompt
String, read-only. The full instructions and question text shown to the learner for the current task.
promptImage
Image, read-only. The chart or diagram provided for the task, when present (e.g., “Hình ảnh đề bài”).
answerInput
String (textarea), required. The learner’s written answer for the current task (placeholder “Nhập bài làm của bạn tại đây...”).
taskWordCount
Number, read-only. The number of words in the current task’s answer (e.g., “0 từ”).
totalWordCount
Number, read-only. The combined word count across all tasks in the exam (e.g., “Tổng số từ cả bài thi”).
taskTarget
String, read-only. The minimum word target for the current task (e.g., “Mục tiêu task này: tối thiểu 150 từ”).
Element
Description
Finish Writing Button (“Hoàn thành Writing”)
Action: Submits all tasks and ends the Writing section. Prompts for confirmation. Also triggered automatically when the countdown timer reaches zero.
Exit Button (“Thoát”)
Action: Exits the writing exam session, with a confirmation prompt to prevent accidental loss of answers.
Task Navigation Tabs (Task 1 / Task 2)
Action: Switches the prompt and answer area to the selected task. Each tab shows that task’s word count.
Previous / Next Task Buttons (← / →)
Action: Navigates to the previous or next task.
Answer Input Field
Action: Allows the learner to type and edit their answer for the current task. The word count updates in real time.
Task Prompt Panel
Display: Shows the task instructions and any accompanying image; scrollable when the content is long.

3.3.1.1.1 Listening Test
Related Use Case(s): UC-58 – Take Placement Test
The Listening Practice screen presents a full IELTS-format listening assessment. The learner listens to an auto-playing audio track and answers questions across multiple parts in real time, under a countdown timer.
The screen provides:
●        An exam header with the test title, a countdown timer, audio playback status, violation counter, and Submit / Exit buttons.
●        A multi-part structure (Part 1–4), each containing a passage title, a listening context description, question groups with instructions, and answer input fields.
●        A bottom question navigation panel showing all question numbers color-coded by status (answered, flagged, unanswered) with part-level progress counts.
●        An audio source label identifying the active audio track.
●        Real-time tracking of answered questions and remaining time.
Field Name
Description
examTitle
String, read-only. The title of the listening practice test displayed in the exam header.
partLabel
String, read-only. The label of the currently selected listening part.
passageTitle
String, read-only. The title of the listening passage or scenario displayed in the content area.
progressInfo
String, read-only. The learner’s answered-question progress for the listening test.
audioSourceLabel
String, read-only. The label identifying the audio source used for the listening section.
countdownTimer
String, read-only. The remaining exam time displayed as a countdown timer.
violationCount
Number, read-only. The number of exam integrity violations recorded during the listening session.
questionGroupLabel
String, read-only. The label for a group of questions within the selected part.
instruction
String, read-only. The instruction text explaining how the learner should answer the question group.
answerInput
String (text input), required. The learner’s typed answer for each fill-in-the-blank question; saved automatically as the learner types.
Element
Description
Audio Status Indicator
Display: Shows that the listening audio is currently playing automatically. It is read-only and does not allow the learner to pause or rewind the audio.
Exit Button
Action: Exits the listening exam session with a confirmation prompt to prevent accidental loss of answers.
Submit Button
Action: Submits all listening answers and ends the exam. A confirmation prompt is shown before submission.
Part Navigation Panel
Action: Switches the main content area to the selected listening part and shows progress for each part.
Flag Question Button
Action: Marks or unmarks the current question for later review. Flagged questions are highlighted in the question navigation panel
Question Number Dots
Display: Shows visual indicators for all question numbers and their current answer status.
Answer Input Field
Action: Allows the learner to type an answer for a fill-in-the-blank question. The answer is saved automatically while typing.
3.3.1.1.2 Writing Test
Related Use Case(s): UC-58 – Take Placement Test
The Writing Practice screen presents a full IELTS-format writing assessment. An enrolled learner reads each task prompt, composes their answer in a dedicated answer area, and moves between tasks, all under a single countdown timer and exam-mode monitoring. The learner can view the task instructions and any accompanying image, type and edit their answer, track the word count against the task target, and submit the whole Writing section when finished.
The screen provides:
●        An exam header with the test title, a countdown timer, a violation counter, and Finish Writing / Exit buttons.
●        A task prompt area (left) showing the task label, title, word-count guidance, the instructions, and any image provided for the task.
●        An answer area (right) with a text area for the response, a live word count, the task’s word target, and the total word count across the exam.
●        A bottom task navigation bar listing each task (Task 1, Task 2) with its word count, plus previous and next task controls.
Field Name
Description
examTitle
String, read-only. The full name of the writing practice test (e.g., “IELTS Placement Test 2025 January Writing Practice Test 1”).
countdownTimer
String, read-only. The remaining exam time displayed as a MM:SS countdown (e.g., “59:00”).
violationCount
Number, read-only. The number of recorded exam integrity violations (e.g., “Vi phạm: 0”).
taskLabel
String, read-only. The label of the current task section (e.g., “PART 1”).
taskTitle
String, read-only. The heading of the current writing task (e.g., “Writing Task 1”).
taskGuidance
String, read-only. The word-count guidance for the task (e.g., “Gợi ý : Tối thiểu 150 từ”).
taskPrompt
String, read-only. The full instructions and question text shown to the learner for the current task.
promptImage
Image, read-only. The chart or diagram provided for the task, when present (e.g., “Hình ảnh đề bài”).
answerInput
String (textarea), required. The learner’s written answer for the current task (placeholder “Nhập bài làm của bạn tại đây...”).
taskWordCount
Number, read-only. The number of words in the current task’s answer (e.g., “0 từ”).
totalWordCount
Number, read-only. The combined word count across all tasks in the exam (e.g., “Tổng số từ cả bài thi”).
taskTarget
String, read-only. The minimum word target for the current task (e.g., “Mục tiêu task này: tối thiểu 150 từ”).
Element
Description
Finish Writing Button (“Hoàn thành Writing”)
Action: Submits all tasks and ends the Writing section. Prompts for confirmation. Also triggered automatically when the countdown timer reaches zero.
Exit Button (“Thoát”)
Action: Exits the writing exam session, with a confirmation prompt to prevent accidental loss of answers.
Task Navigation Tabs (Task 1 / Task 2)
Action: Switches the prompt and answer area to the selected task. Each tab shows that task’s word count.
Previous / Next Task Buttons (← / →)
Action: Navigates to the previous or next task.
Answer Input Field
Action: Allows the learner to type and edit their answer for the current task. The word count updates in real time.
Task Prompt Panel
Display: Shows the task instructions and any accompanying image; scrollable when the content is long.
3.3.1.1.3 Reading Test
Related Use Case(s): UC-58 – Take Placement Test
The Reading Practice screen presents a full IELTS-format reading assessment. An enrolled learner reads each passage on the left, answers the associated questions on the right, and moves between parts using the bottom navigation bar, while an exam header tracks the remaining time and any exam-integrity violations.
The screen provides:
●    An exam header with the test title, a countdown timer, a violation counter, and Finish Reading / Exit buttons.
●    A reading passage panel (left) showing the part label, passage title, time guidance, and the full passage text organized into labelled paragraphs (A, B, C, …).
●    A question panel (right) grouping the questions (e.g., paragraph matching and sentence completion) with their instructions and answer inputs.
●    A bottom part-navigation bar listing each part (Part 1–3) with its answered-question count and clickable question numbers color-coded by status.
Field Name
Description
examTitle
String, read-only. The full name of the reading practice test displayed in the exam header (e.g., “IELTS Mock Test 2025 January Reading Practice Test 1”).
countdownTimer
String, read-only. The remaining exam time displayed as an MM:SS countdown (e.g., “59:54”).
violationCount
Number, read-only. The number of recorded exam integrity violations (e.g., “Vi phạm: 0”).
partLabel
String, read-only. The label of the currently selected reading part (e.g., “PART 1”).
passageTitle
String, read-only. The title of the reading passage displayed above the passage text (e.g., “Money Transfers by Mobile”).
passageInstruction
String, read-only. The time-guidance text for the passage (e.g., “You should spend about 20 minutes on Questions 1-13.”).
passageContent
String, read-only. The full reading passage text, organized into labelled paragraphs (A, B, C, …).
questionGroupLabel
String, read-only. The label for a group of questions within the selected part (e.g., “Questions 1-4”, “Questions 5-8”).
questionInstruction
String, read-only. The instruction text explaining how to answer each question group (e.g., “Which paragraph contains each of the following pieces of information?”).
dropdownAnswer
String (selection), required. The learner’s selected paragraph/option for a matching question (e.g., Q1–Q4).
answerInput
String (text input), required. The learner’s typed answer for a sentence-completion / gap-fill question (e.g., Q5–Q8).
progressInfo
String, read-only. The answered-question progress for each part shown in the navigation bar (e.g., “0 of 13 questions”).
Elements
Description
Exit Button (“Thoát”)
Action: Exits the reading exam session, with a confirmation prompt to prevent accidental loss of answers.
Finish Reading Button (“Hoàn thành phần Reading”)
Action: Submits all reading answers and ends the Reading section. A confirmation prompt is shown before submission.
Passage Panel
Display: Shows the reading passage title, time guidance, and full passage text; scrollable when the content exceeds the panel height.
Question Panel
Display: Shows each question group with its instruction and the corresponding answer inputs; scrollable independently from the passage.
Answer Dropdown
Action: Lets the learner choose the matching paragraph/option for a matching-type question.
Answer Input Field
Action: Allows the learner to type an answer for a sentence-completion / gap-fill question. The value is auto-saved as the learner types.
Part Navigation Bar
Action: Switches the passage and questions to the selected part (Part 1–3) and shows the answered-question count for each part.
Question Number Buttons
Action/Display: Show all question numbers grouped by part and let the learner jump directly to a specific question; color-coded by answered/unanswered status.
3.3.1.1.4 Speaking Test
Related Use Case(s): UC-58 – Take Placement Test
The Speaking Practice screen presents a full IELTS-format speaking assessment. The learner reads the questions and cue card for all three parts on the left and records a single spoken answer (with an optional backup transcript) on the right, while an exam header tracks the remaining time and any exam-integrity violations.
The screen provides:
●    An exam header with the test title, a countdown timer, a violation counter, and Submit Exam / Exit buttons.
●    A question panel (left) listing Part 1 (Introduction and Interview), Part 2 (Cue Card), and Part 3 (Discussion) with their questions and guiding points.
●    A speaking recording panel (right) with a single recorder for the whole test, a recording-status indicator, and an audio upload option.
●    An optional backup transcript area with a live word counter, allowing the learner to submit by audio, transcript, or both.
Field Name
Description
examTitle
String, read-only. The full name of the speaking mock test displayed in the exam header (e.g., “IELTS Speaking Mock Test 1”).
countdownTimer
String, read-only. The remaining exam time displayed as an MM:SS countdown (e.g., “14:38”).
violationCount
Number, read-only. The number of recorded exam integrity violations (e.g., “Vi phạm: 2”).
partLabel
String, read-only. The label of each speaking part (e.g., “PART 1 · INTRODUCTION AND INTERVIEW”, “PART 2 · CUE CARD”, “PART 3 · DISCUSSION”).
questionList
String, read-only. The list of interview / discussion questions shown for Part 1 and Part 3.
cueCardPrompt
String, read-only. The Part 2 cue-card topic and its guiding bullet points (e.g., “Describe a leisure activity you enjoy.”).
recordingStatus
String, read-only. The current recording state indicator (e.g., “Chưa có bản ghi” – no recording yet).
recordingHint
String, read-only. Guidance text explaining that a single recording covers the whole speaking test and that the device check does not reappear.
transcriptInput
String (textarea), optional. The learner’s optional backup transcript of the main ideas of the speaking answer.
transcriptWordCount
Number, read-only. The number of words entered in the backup transcript (e.g., “Số từ transcript: 0”).
Element
Description
Exit Button (“Thoát”)
Action: Exits the speaking exam session, with a confirmation prompt to prevent accidental loss of the recording.
Submit Exam Button (“Nộp toàn bộ bài thi”)
Action: Submits the entire speaking test (audio recording and/or transcript) and ends the session. A confirmation prompt is shown before submission.
Question Panel
Display: Shows all three speaking parts with their questions and the Part 2 cue card; scrollable when the content exceeds the panel height.
Start Recording Button (“Bắt đầu ghi âm”)
Action: Starts recording the learner’s spoken answer for the whole test using the microphone; toggles to stop the recording.
Recording Status Indicator (“Chưa có bản ghi”)
Display: Shows whether an audio recording has been made or uploaded yet.
Upload Recording Control (“Chưa tải bản ghi nào”)
Action/Display: Lets the learner upload an audio file and shows the uploaded recording status.
Transcript Input Field (“TRANSCRIPT DỰ PHÒNG”)
Action: Allows the learner to type an optional backup transcript of the key ideas of their answer.
Transcript Word Counter (“Số từ transcript”)
Display: Shows the live word count of the backup transcript.

Description

errorCorrectionExamples

originalSentence

correctedSentence

explanation

3.3.2.3 Auto Grading

The Auto Grading screen presents learners with an automated, AI-generated evaluation report of their submission. It highlights an estimated score, evaluation status, and breakdown reviews categorized by specific evaluation criteria such as semantic accuracy, collocations, sentence quality, and topic relevance.
Field Name
Description
estimatedScore
Text Field. Displays the estimated overall band score or score range calculated by the AI engine.
evaluationStatus
Status Tag. Indicates the current stage of the grading process (e.g., Completed, In Progress, Draft).
criteriaReviewHeader
Section Header. Title for the main content block containing criteria-based feedback cards.
semanticAccuracy
Text Block Container. Displays the automated feedback regarding how accurately the user's input conveys the intended meaning.
naturalCollocations
Text Block Container. Evaluates and reviews the natural use of collocations, idioms, and word combinations in the submission.
sentenceQuality
Text Block Container. Focuses on grammatical structures, syntax complexity, and overall sentence formation quality.
topicRelevance
Text Block Container. Analyzes how well the content aligns with and responds to the given test topic/prompt.
Element
Description
Score Highlight Badge
Visual Highlight: A colored tag designed to immediately capture the user's attention regarding their overall performance score.
Status Pill
Status Indicator: A pill-shaped element displaying the system status of the assessment report.
Criteria Feedback Cards
Content Containers: Separate cards enclosing text lines for each grading metric, ensuring the detailed feedback is structured and easily readable.

3.4.9 Approval Workflows
3.4.9.1 Curriculum Approval
Curriculum Approval lets Staffs approve or reject offline/virtual curriculum program versions submitted by Content Managers.
The screen provides:
●    Pending-approval counter.
●    Reload action for the approval queue.
●    Empty state when no curricula are waiting review.
Field Name
Description
pendingCount
Number, read-only. Curricula waiting approval.
programTitle
String, read-only. Offline/virtual program name.
programMode
String, read-only. OFFLINE / VIRTUAL.
decision
String, required. Approved / Rejected when reviewing.
reviewNote
String, optional. Reviewer note.
Element
Description
Pending Counter Card
Display: Number of curricula awaiting review.
Reload Button
Action: Refreshes the approval queue.
Approve / Reject Action
Action: Finalizes curriculum review (when items exist).
3.4.9.2 Online Course Approval
Related Use Case(s): UC-87 – View Online Course; UC-88 – Update Online Course; UC-89 – Deactivate Online Course
Online Course Approval is the Manager Review Gate before a course goes live. Managers review courses submitted by Content Managers and track Pending / Published / Rejected counts.
The screen provides:
●    Manager Review Gate banner with Pending / Published / Rejected counters.
●    Search by course title / slug / category and status filter.
●    Empty clean-queue state when nothing is pending under the current filter.
Field Name
Description
pendingCount
Number, read-only. Courses waiting manager approval.
publishedCount
Number, read-only. Already published courses.
rejectedCount
Number, read-only. Rejected submissions.
searchKeyword
String, optional. Course / slug / category search.
statusFilter
String, optional. Pending / Published / Rejected.
Element
Description
Status Counters
Display: Queue health for course approvals.
Search Box
Action: Finds courses by title, slug or category.
Status Filter Dropdown
Action: Narrows the approval list.
Refresh Button
Action: Reloads the approval queue.

Description

classroomOrProgram

decision
String, required. Approved / Rejected when reviewing.

Description

3.4.9.4 Staff Enrollment Request Queue
Related Use Case(s): UC-131 – View Class Registration Requests; UC-132 – Schedule Test for Applicant; UC-133 – Record Applicant Test Result; UC-134 – Reject Class Registration; UC-135 – Approve and Assign Class; UC-136 – Confirm Learner Registration; UC-137 – Record Tuition Payment; UC-138 – Confirm Tuition Payment Proof; UC-139 – Reject Tuition Payment Proof
Staff review consultation forms submitted from Opening Schedule. They can complete consultation, assign class, reject or filter by lifecycle status (Mới đăng ký / Đã tư vấn / Đã xếp lớp).
The screen provides:
●    Status tabs and keyword search.
●    Request table with learner contact and consultation details.
●    Actions: Ghi nhận tư vấn xong, Xếp lớp, Từ chối.
Field Name
Description
status
Enum, filter, optional. Tab values: SUBMITTED (Mới đăng ký), WAITING_FOR_CLASS (Đã tư vấn), CLASS_ASSIGNED or REJECTED.
contactName
String, read-only. Learner name from the consultation form.
contactEmail
String, read-only. Learner email used for follow-up contact.
consultationTrack
String, read-only. Target program selected by the learner (e.g., IELTS, TOEIC).
preferredSchedule
String, read-only. Learner preferred study schedule (e.g., “Tối 2-4-6”).
note
String, textarea, optional. Staff note recorded when consultation is completed.
Element
Description
Status tabs
Action: Switches between Mới đăng ký, Đã tư vấn and Đã xếp lớp queues.
'Ghi nhận tư vấn xong' Button
Action: Marks a submitted request as consulted and moves it to WAITING_FOR_CLASS.
'Xếp lớp' Button
Action: Assigns the learner to an opening classroom and closes the request as CLASS_ASSIGNED.
'Từ chối' Button
Action: Rejects the enrollment request with a mandatory reason.
Keyword search
Action: Filters the request list by learner name, email or phone.
3.4.9.5 Staff Classroom Opening Proposals
Related Use Case(s): UC-122 – Create Classroom Proposal; UC-123 – Update Classroom Proposal; UC-124 – Submit Classroom Proposal; UC-125 – View Classroom Proposal
Staff prepare class-opening proposals before manager approval. The page manages DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, REJECTED and APPROVED proposals with a scheduling modal.
The screen provides:
●    Status tabs: Bản nháp / Chờ Manager duyệt / Bị từ chối / Đã duyệt.
●    Proposal form: title, program, capacity, schedule, teacher, room.
●    Submit for manager approval action.
Field Name
Description
title
String, input, required. Proposed class title shown on the proposal card (e.g., “IELTS Intermediate – Khai giảng tháng 8/2026”).
courseOfferingId
Number, select, required. Offline or virtual training program selected for the new class.
capacity
Number, input, required. Maximum number of learners for the proposed class.
plannedStartDate
Date, input, required. Planned opening date of the class.
plannedEndDate
Date, input, required. Planned end date of the class.
approvalStatus
Enum, read-only. Lifecycle state: DRAFT, PENDING_APPROVAL, REJECTED or APPROVED.
Element
Description
Status tabs
Action: Switches between Bản nháp, Chờ Manager duyệt, Bị từ chối and Đã duyệt proposal lists.
'Tạo đề xuất' Button
Action: Opens the proposal modal to create a new draft class-opening plan.
'Gửi Manager duyệt' Button
Action: Submits a draft proposal to the manager approval queue.
'Chỉnh sửa' Button
Action: Edits a draft or rejected proposal before resubmission.
3.4.9.6 Manager Classroom Proposal Approval
Related Use Case(s): UC-142 – View Classroom Proposals; UC-143 – Approve Classroom Proposal; UC-144 – Reject Classroom Proposal
Managers review staff proposals and approve or reject them. Approved proposals create official classrooms.
The screen provides:
●    Tabs: Chờ duyệt / Đã duyệt / Đã từ chối.
●    Proposal detail panel with schedule, teacher and capacity.
●    Approve and reject actions with reason.
Field Name
Description
approvalStatus
Enum, filter, optional. Tab values: PENDING_APPROVAL (Chờ duyệt), APPROVED or REJECTED.
proposalCode
String, read-only. System-generated proposal identifier (e.g., “CP-2026-0007”).
title
String, read-only. Proposed class title submitted by staff.
courseOfferingTitle
String, read-only. Training program associated with the proposal.
capacity
Number, read-only. Proposed learner capacity.
rejectionReason
String, textarea, required when rejecting. Manager must explain why the proposal is rejected.
Element
Description
Status tabs
Action: Switches between Chờ duyệt, Đã duyệt and Đã từ chối proposal queues.
'Duyệt và tạo lớp chính thức' Button
Action: Approves the proposal and creates the official classroom offering.
'Từ chối đề xuất' Button
Action: Rejects the proposal and returns it to staff with a reason.

Statistics Badge. Counts items needing immediate instructor action.

Statistics Badge. The number of classroom sessions already conducted.
allowResubmission
Statistics Badge. The number of scheduled future classroom sessions.

Information: Displays the class name and core statistics as background context.

Navigation: Standard class management menu; “Tài liệu / Bài tập” is the active context.

Description

Accepts valid alphanumeric text matching communication routing parameters.
phoneNumberInput
Accepts numerical sequences representing user call destinations.
targetScoreInput
Accepts defined exam targets or required benchmark milestones.
currentScoreInput
Accepts numerical parameters charting current entry diagnostics.
targetNotesInput
Accepts narrative context notes on academic goal parameters or weaknesses.
saveProfileBtn
Validates profile metadata alterations and updates database registry fields.
uploadAvatarBtn
Spawns system file selectors to upload and bind image attachments as user profile pictures.
oldPasswordInput
Obscures alphanumeric entries verifying user authorization status.
newPasswordInput
Obscures character entries to safely input the updated security key string.
confirmPassInput
Obscures character entries to confirm matching structural integrity of the security key string.
changePasswordBtn
Triggers immediate credential record rotation validations against authentication databases.
toggleTwoFactorMfa
Interchanges operational statuses between disabled and authorized multi-factor steps.
profileSectionCard
Upper central workspace organizing basic contact data form elements.
securitySectionCard
Lower central workspace grouping system access controls and verification tools.

3.8.2.2 Homework Management
Related Use Case(s): UC-67 – Create Homework; UC-68 – View Homework; UC-69 – Update Homework; UC-70 – Delete Homework; UC-71 – View Homework Submissions; UC-72 – Grade Homework Submission
This interface enables instructors to manage all writing and practice assignments for a specific class. Teachers can monitor submission and grading statistics, create new assignments, publish homework, and perform actions like grading, editing, or deleting active tasks.
Field Name
Description
teachingContext
Navigation context label indicating the teacher's workspace.
rosterRatioCount
Status badge indicating the offline/on-site nature of the class.
attentionFlagsCount
Status badge showing that the course is currently active/in progress.
pendingGradingCount
Title Text. The unique identifier/name of the specific class group.
completedLessonsCount
Statistics Badge. Displays current enrolled students over total capacity.
upcomingLessonsCount
Statistics Badge. Counts items needing immediate instructor action.
workspaceTitle
Statistics Badge. Total number of student submissions awaiting evaluation.
workspaceSubtitle
Statistics Badge. The number of classroom sessions already conducted.
assignmentName
Statistics Badge. The number of scheduled future classroom sessions.
assignmentPrompt
Section Header. Title for the core homework management container.
deadlineLabel
Description Text. Explains that homework scores will automatically update the class gradebook.
submittedCount
Status Tag. Indicates that the assignment is currently open for student submissions.
awaitingGradingText
Configuration Tag. Shows the evaluation method set for this task (Manual Teacher Grading).
maxScoreLabel
Score Field. Indicates the maximum point scale achievable for the task.
Element
Description
assignTaskTopButton
Action: Shortcut button in the header zone to assign quick homework.
scheduleTeachingButton
Action: Navigation button redirecting the teacher to their teaching calendar.
navigationTabs
Navigation Menu: A tab bar allowing the instructor to toggle between different class dimensions. The “Tài liệu / Bài tập” context is highlighted.
createNewAssignmentBtn
Action: Secondary CTA button inside the management card to launch the assignment creation form modal.
gradeSubmissionButton
Action: Main action button on the assignment card. Redirects the instructor to the student submissions evaluation pipeline.
editAssignmentButton
Action: Opens the configuration form to modify the title, description, or deadline of the selected assignment.

detailPanel
Shows detailed information and available actions for the selected record.
paginationControls
Moves between result pages without changing the active filters.
3.10.4 Backup and Recovery
3.10.4.1 Backup and Restore
Related Use Case(s): N/A – No corresponding use case is defined in the current Use Case Catalog.
The screen enables an administrator to create database backups and review restore readiness. It brings the information, status indicators, and actions required for this workflow into one focused view. The mockup reflects the current EnglishLab implementation and the role-specific permissions applied by the system.
The screen provides:
● Database connectivity and backup capability status.
● Backup history and verification state.
● Create-backup action.
● Restore or download actions when a backup is available.
Field Name
Description
pageTitle
String, read-only. Primary title identifying the current screen and workflow.
searchQuery
String, optional. Text used to narrow records displayed on the screen.
statusFilter
Enum, optional. Current status filter applied to the result set.
recordSummary
Object, read-only. Aggregate totals and key status counts for the workflow.
selectedRecord
Object, read-only. Record currently opened for review or action.
Element
Description
filterBar
Groups search, status, type, and refresh controls for the current list.
recordList
Displays the records or cards available in the current workflow.
primaryActionBtn
Starts the principal create, submit, or processing action.
detailPanel
Shows detailed information and available actions for the selected record.
paginationControls
Moves between result pages without changing the active filters.
4. Non-Functional Requirements
4.1 External Interfaces
No
Category
Requirement
1
User Interfaces
-  All texts and messages in the system are displayed in the Vietnamese language.
-  All pages must have a consistent visual theme, color palette, and typeface.
-  The UI must be responsive, ensuring optimal display on different screen sizes (Desktops, Laptops, and Tablets, mobile).
-  The system must ask for confirmation (Y/N) for data deletion and critical operations.
-  Active functions on the navigation bar must be highlighted to inform users of their current location.
-  For pages with large amounts of data, the system must support multi-criteria search to narrow down results.
- The web interface must be compatible with popular browsers such as Chrome, Firefox, Safari, and Edge.
2
Software Interfaces
- The system shall interface with a PostgreSQL database management system for secure data storage and retrieval.
- The system will integrate with the PayOS payment gateway to handle online transactions (payment initiation, result processing, and inquiry).
- The system must integrate with an Email Service (SMTP) to send automated notifications like password resets or payment receipts.
3
Communication Interfaces
- The system will employ secure HTTPS protocols (TLS 1.2 or higher) to ensure encrypted communication between the client and server.
- Data exchange between the Frontend and Backend via RESTful APIs must use the JSON format.
- All API requests must be authenticated using JSON Web Tokens (JWT) to prevent unauthorized access
4.2 Quality Attributes
No
Category
Requirement
1
Performance
- UI Response Time: Use testing tools (e.g., Apache JMeter) to ensure that the response time for typical user interactions is under 2 seconds under normal load conditions.
- Concurrency: The system must be capable of handling at least 1,000 concurrent user interactions without degradation in performance or data integrity.
- Payment Processing: The integration with PayOS should ensure that payment status updates are reflected in the system within 5 seconds after receiving the gateway's callback.
2
Security
- Data Encryption: All sensitive data transmission must be secured using HTTPS protocols with SSL/TLS encryption.
- Password Hashing: User passwords must be securely hashed using a strong algorithm (e.g., BCrypt or SHA-512) before being stored in the database.
- Authentication: The system must use JSON Web Tokens (JWT) for authenticating API requests. Tokens must be validated for every protected resource access to prevent unauthorized entry.
- The system should implement role-based access control (RBAC) for user and admin actions.
3
Usability
- The system must comply with modern web GUI standards to ensure a short learning curve for new users.
- The system should provide hover tooltips and inline help for complex features or technical terms to assist users without external documentation.
- The workflow for complex tasks, such as creating a course or processing a refund, must be intuitive and follow a logical step-by-step process.
4
Compatibility
- The system should be compatible with major web browsers (Chrome, Firefox, Safari, Edge) and different devices (laptops, desktops, tablets).
- Responsive design for optimal display on desktops, tablets.
5
Maintainability
- The code should follow coding standards and best practices to enhance maintainability.
- The system should be well-documented to facilitate future updates and bug fixes.
5. Requirement Appendix
5.1 Business Rules
ID
Rule Definition
BR-01
Passwords must be at least 8 characters long and contain uppercase, lowercase, numbers, and special characters.
BR-02
An email address can only be associated with one active account in the system.
BR-03
User accounts are automatically locked after 5 consecutive failed login attempts (applies to standard email/password login).
BR-04
System-generated OTPs for password recovery expire after 5 minutes from the time of request.
BR-05
Learners can only enroll in courses if the course status is "Active", capacity limit is not reached, and they are not already enrolled.
BR-06
The OTP entered by the user must exactly match the system-generated OTP and must not be expired.
BR-07
Uploaded files (for homework or assignments) must not exceed 20MB and must be in PDF, DOCX, JPG, or PNG formats.
BR-08
Timed practice exercises and placement tests must auto-submit immediately when the countdown timer reaches zero.
BR-09
Audio recordings for speaking practice must not exceed 5 minutes in length.
BR-10
Learners can only download learning materials that belong to a Teacher-led Classroom.
BR-11
Teachers can only manage attendance, homework, and practice contents for the classrooms they are explicitly assigned to.
BR-12
Class attendance can only be marked or updated on or after the scheduled date of the class session.
BR-13
Audio files uploaded by Teachers for listening practices must be in MP3/WAV format and not exceed 20MB.
BR-14
A Curriculumn or online course cannot be deleted if it has active enrolled learners or is linked to an active entity.
BR-15
Online Course names and Classroom names must be unique within the system.
BR-16
A teacher cannot be assigned to a classroom if their teaching schedule conflicts with the classroom's timetable.
BR-17
The number of learners assigned to a classroom cannot exceed its maximum capacity.
BR-18
User accounts cannot be permanently deleted from the database to maintain data integrity; they can only be locked or deactivated.
BR-19
A support ticket can only be marked as "Resolved/Closed" after at least one reply has been sent by the admin/manager.
BR-20
A course can appear at most once in a learner's wishlist and at most once in the shopping cart; a course the learner is already enrolled in cannot be added to the wishlist or the cart.
BR-21
Only courses with status "Active"/"Published" and a valid price (including free courses with price = 0) can be added to the cart or purchased at checkout.
BR-22
For paid courses, enrollment is granted only after the payment is successfully confirmed by the PayOS payment gateway; free courses (price = 0) are enrolled immediately without payment.
BR-23
Upon successful checkout, the purchased courses are removed from the learner's cart and a payment receipt is sent to the learner's registered email.
BR-24
Only authorized Content Managers can create, view, update, or delete placement exam content.
BR-25
Each placement exam question must belong to exactly one valid skill section (Listening, Reading, Writing, or Speaking).
BR-26
Objective placement exam questions must have at least two answer options and at least one correct answer.
BR-27
Writing and Speaking placement exam questions must include a clear prompt or task instruction.
BR-28
The score value of a placement exam question must be a positive number greater than zero.
BR-29
Learners must not be able to view the correct answers before or during a placement exam; correct answers are visible only to authorized management users.
BR-30
Viewing placement exam content must not modify any data, and displayed lists must reflect the latest available exam/question data.
BR-31
Placement exam content that has already been used in learner attempts must not be hard-deleted; it must be deactivated instead, and historical learner results must be preserved.
BR-32
Deleting placement exam content requires confirmation; deleted or inactive questions must not appear in new learner attempts, and their related answer key and score configuration must be removed or deactivated.
BR-33
If a create or update operation fails validation, no invalid placement exam data is saved and the previously valid version remains unchanged.
BR-34
Visible discussions belonging to a published online course may be viewed without authentication, but write interactions require authentication and valid course enrollment.
BR-35
A discussion title must contain 8–180 characters; question content must contain 20–3000 characters; reply content must contain 2–3000 characters.
BR-36
Only Learners with ACTIVE or COMPLETED course enrollment, or authorized moderator roles, may create or interact with course discussions.
BR-37
A Learner cannot mark their own reply as helpful.
BR-38
Only the thread author or an authorized Admin, Manager, or Content Manager may mark a discussion as RESOLVED.
BR-39
Content matching configured prohibited phrases is stored with PENDING_REVIEW status and masked until reviewed.
BR-40
HIDDEN discussions and replies are excluded from normal lists and cannot receive new replies or reactions.
BR-41
A Learner may report a specific discussion thread or reply at most once.
BR-42
Every report must contain a supported reason category; OTHER requires a non-empty explanation no longer than 500 characters.
BR-43
Reporting requires the same authenticated enrollment access as other discussion write operations.
BR-44
A thread or reply reaching three reports is automatically changed to PENDING_REVIEW, except that a RESOLVED thread retains RESOLVED status.
BR-45
Reporting does not automatically hide content; only an authorized Content Manager moderation action may hide it.
BR-46
A report that has already been processed cannot be processed again.
BR-47
Lesson notes are private and all server list, update, and delete operations must be scoped to the authenticated Learner.
BR-48
Note content is required and may contain at most 2000 characters.
BR-49
selectedText is optional and may contain at most 2000 characters.
BR-50
transcriptStartSeconds is optional but, when supplied, must be greater than or equal to zero.
BR-51
Creating a note requires valid learning access to the associated course and the lesson must belong to that course.
BR-52
Updating a note requires both note ownership and current course learning access.
BR-53
The current implementation permits deletion of an owned note without revalidating course enrollment.
BR-54
Multiple notes for the same Learner and lesson are allowed.
BR-55
Unauthenticated notes are stored only in browser localStorage and are not automatically migrated to the server after login.
BR-56
The workspace manually limits note content to 800 characters, while the backend contract permits up to 2000 characters; these limits should be aligned.
BR-57
In sequential online courses, a Learner may open a lesson only when the previous lesson is completed; System stores lesson progress as NOT_STARTED, IN_PROGRESS, or COMPLETED.
BR-58
Each online course module ends with a module test (final practice exercise). When the course defines a target score, the Learner must score at least (target score − 0.5) on the module test to pass; only then is the next module and its lessons unlocked. Failing below that threshold requires retaking the module test.
BR-59
Classroom practice exercises are voluntary learning activities separate from graded homework; completing practice does not replace homework submission requirements.
BR-60
A Learner may track class registration requests through defined statuses (for example SUBMITTED, TEST_SCHEDULED, CLASS_ASSIGNED, REJECTED, CANCELLED) and may cancel only while the request is still pending staff decision.
BR-61
Tuition payment proof uploads must not exceed 20MB and must be PDF, JPG, or PNG; only the owning Learner and authorized staff may view submitted proofs.
BR-62
Flashcard progress (starred/learned status) is private to the Learner and scoped to the vocabulary term and selected source context.
BR-63
Learners may view practice attempt history only for their own attempts; attempt records include submission time, score, and evaluation status.
